3a:["$","ul","ul-5",{"children":["\n",["$","li","li-0",{"children":["\n",["$","p","p-0",{"children":"最终将图像缩减成了只有 3 种颜色"}],"\n"]}],"\n",["$","li","li-1",{"children":["\n",["$","p","p-0",{"children":"Color Quantization is the process of reducing number of colors in an image.
